```css
/* =========================================================
   ADDITIVE FEATURE STYLES (NEW)
========================================================= */

/* Field Reports Cards Grid */
.field-reports-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr));
  gap: 16px;
  padding: 16px;
}

.field-card {
  background: var(--surface-2);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 12px;
  overflow: hidden;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  box-shadow: var(--shadow);
  transition: transform 0.2s ease;
}

.field-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
}

.field-card-header {
  padding: 10px 14px;
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
}

.field-badge {
  padding: 3px 8px;
  border-radius: 4px;
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 800;
  color: #fff;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.badge-severe { background: #ff0055; }
.badge-high { background: #ff9900; }
.badge-moderate { background: #c58a27; }
.badge-low { background: #4f8a5b; }

.field-type {
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 700;
  margin-left: 6px;
}

.field-time {
  font-size: 10px;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.field-card-body {
  padding: 12px;
  display: flex;
  gap: 12px;
}

.field-photo-box {
  width: 80px;
  height: 80px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  border-radius: 8px;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: #000;
}

.field-photo-box img {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  object-fit: cover;
}

.field-details h4 {
  font-size: 13px;
  margin-bottom: 4px;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.field-details p {
  font-size: 11px;
  color: var(--muted);
  line-height: 1.3;
  margin-bottom: 6px;
}

.field-meta {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  font-size: 9px;
  color: var(--muted);
}

/* Modal Dialog Styles */
.modal-overlay {
  position: fixed;
  top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0;
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.75);
  backdrop-filter: blur(5px);
  z-index: 9999;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 16px;
}

.modal-card {
  background: var(--surface);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 16px;
  width: 100%;
  max-width: 550px;
  box-shadow: 0 20px 50px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);
  overflow: hidden;
}

.modal-header {
  padding: 18px 20px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
}

.modal-header h3 {
  font-size: 15px;
  color: var(--text);
}

.modal-close {
  background: none;
  border: none;
  font-size: 18px;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.modal-body {
  padding: 20px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 16px;
}

.modal-input {
  width: 100%;
  background: var(--surface-2);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 10px;
  color: var(--text);
  font-size: 13px;
  font-family: inherit;
}

.recipient-checklist {
  max-height: 150px;
  overflow-y: auto;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 8px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
}

.checkbox-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 6px 4px;
  font-size: 12px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.modal-footer {
  padding: 14px 20px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--border);
  display: flex;
  justify-content: flex-end;
  gap: 10px;
}

.btn-secondary {
  background: var(--surface-2);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  color: var(--text);
  padding: 8px 14px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.btn-primary-danger {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #c74a45, #d84c4c);
  border: none;
  color: #fff;
  padding: 8px 16px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.btn-danger-glow {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #c74a45, #e63946) !important;
  color: white !important;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(230, 57, 70, 0.4);
}

.status-report-box {
  padding: 10px 14px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  font-size: 12px;
}

.status-success { background: rgba(79, 138, 91, 0.15); border: 1px solid var(--green); color: var(--green); }
.status-error { background: rgba(199, 74, 69, 0.15); border: 1px solid var(--red); color: var(--red); }

/* Audit Table */
.audit-table {
  width: 100%;
  border-collapse: collapse;
  font-size: 12px;
}

.audit-table th, .audit-table td {
  padding: 10px 12px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
  text-align: left;
}

.audit-table th {
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 10px;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.msg-truncate {
  max-width: 250px;
  white-space: nowrap;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
}

/* ML Badge */
.ml-badge {
  background: rgba(111, 133, 57, 0.15);
  border: 1px solid var(--primary-light);
  color: var(--primary-light);
  padding: 6px 12px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 700;
  margin-bottom: 12px;
  text-align: center;
}

/* Settings Layout */
.settings-grid {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 20px;
  padding: 20px;
}

.settings-card {
  background: var(--surface-2);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 12px;
  padding: 18px;
}

.settings-card h3 {
  font-size: 14px;
  margin-bottom: 12px;
}

.contact-form .input-sm {
  background: var(--surface);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 8px 12px;
  color: var(--text);
  font-size: 12px;
}

.btn-sm-primary {
  background: var(--primary);
  color: white;
  border: none;
  padding: 8px 14px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  font-weight: 700;
  font-size: 12px;
}

.btn-delete-contact {
  background: rgba(199, 74, 69, 0.15);
  color: var(--red);
  border: 1px solid rgba(199, 74, 69, 0.3);
  padding: 4px 8px;
  border-radius: 4px;
  font-size: 10px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

/* Live Toast */
.live-toast {
  position: fixed;
  bottom: 24px; right: 24px;
  background: rgba(20, 28, 23, 0.95);
  border: 1px solid var(--primary-light);
  color: #fff;
  padding: 14px 18px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
  z-index: 10000;
  max-width: 320px;
  animation: slideUp 0.3s ease;
}

/* SMS Gateway Badges & Status */
.gateway-status-pill {
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  padding: 4px 10px;
  border-radius: 20px;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
}
.pill-warning {
  background: rgba(230, 180, 50, 0.15);
  border: 1px solid rgba(230, 180, 50, 0.4);
  color: #f0c050;
}
.pill-success {
  background: rgba(79, 138, 91, 0.15);
  border: 1px solid rgba(79, 138, 91, 0.4);
  color: var(--green);
}

.gateway-badge {
  display: inline-block;
  font-size: 0.7rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  padding: 2px 8px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  margin-left: 8px;
  vertical-align: middle;
}
.badge-sim {
  background: rgba(230, 180, 50, 0.2);
  color: #f0c050;
  border: 1px solid rgba(230, 180, 50, 0.4);
}
.badge-live {
  background: rgba(79, 138, 91, 0.2);
  color: var(--green);
  border: 1px solid rgba(79, 138, 91, 0.4);
}

/* Heatmap Gradient Intensity Scale */
.heatmap-scale-bar {
  position: absolute;
  left: 14px;
  bottom: 14px;
  z-index: 10;
  background: rgba(14, 21, 17, 0.92);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 12px;
  padding: 10px 14px;
  box-shadow: 0 8px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45);
  backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
  color: #fff;
  font-size: 11px;
  max-width: 240px;
}

.heat-gradient-line {
  height: 10px;
  border-radius: 5px;
  margin: 6px 0;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #39ff14 0%, #ffd000 35%, #ff7700 65%, #ff1e1e 88%, #9e0018 100%);
  box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255, 100, 0, 0.35);
}

.heat-labels {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #c0ccc3;
}

.map-layer-toggles {
  display: flex;
  gap: 6px;
  margin-top: 8px;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.layer-chip {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15);
  color: #fff;
  padding: 3px 8px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 600;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: 0.2s ease;
}

.layer-chip.active {
  background: var(--primary);
  border-color: var(--primary-light);
}

.maplibregl-popup-content {
  background: #151e18 !important;
  color: #edf2ed !important;
  border: 1px solid #29362d !important;
  border-radius: 12px !important;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 35px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6) !important;
  padding: 12px 14px !important;
  font-family: inherit !important;
}
.maplibregl-popup-close-button {
  color: #aaa !important;
}
.maplibregl-popup-anchor-bottom .maplibregl-popup-tip {
  border-top-color: #151e18 !important;
}

/* =========================================================
   MERGED OVERVIEW 3D MAP OVERLAY STYLING
========================================================= */
#overviewViewmodePills {
  position: absolute;
  top: 14px;
  left: 50%;
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  z-index: 10;
}

#overviewCompassWidget {
  position: absolute;
  top: 14px;
  right: 16px;
  z-index: 10;
}

#overviewHeatmapLegend {
  position: absolute;
  bottom: 48px;
  right: 16px;
  z-index: 12;
}

#overviewCoordHud {
  position: absolute;
  bottom: 12px;
  right: 16px;
  z-index: 10;
}

#overviewScalebar {
  position: absolute;
  bottom: 12px;
  left: 16px;
  z-index: 10;
}

@media (max-width: 900px) {
  #overviewViewmodePills {
    top: 48px;
    left: 14px;
    transform: none;
  }
  #overviewHeatmapLegend {
    right: 14px;
    bottom: 80px;
  }
  #overviewCoordHud {
    display: none;
  }
  #overviewScalebar {
    display: none;
  }
}

/* Road Connectivity Panel & Badges */
.road-connectivity-status {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 12px 14px;
  background: rgba(79, 138, 91, 0.1);
  border: 1px solid rgba(79, 138, 91, 0.25);
  border-radius: 10px;
  margin-bottom: 14px;
}

.connectivity-indicator {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
}

.connectivity-dot {
  width: 10px;
  height: 10px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: #4f8a5b;
  box-shadow: 0 0 10px #4f8a5b;
}

.road-badge {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4px;
  padding: 3px 8px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.road-badge-passable {
  background: rgba(79, 138, 91, 0.18);
  color: #62c776;
  border: 1px solid rgba(79, 138, 91, 0.35);
}

.road-badge-watch {
  background: rgba(197, 138, 39, 0.18);
  color: #f0be52;
  border: 1px solid rgba(197, 138, 39, 0.35);
}

.road-badge-caution {
  background: rgba(199, 74, 69, 0.18);
  color: #ff6b6b;
  border: 1px solid rgba(199, 74, 69, 0.35);
}

.road-highway-tag {
  color: var(--primary-light);
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 700;
  margin-right: 4px;
}

.road-card-interactive {
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all 0.25s ease;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
}

.road-card-interactive:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08) !important;
  border-color: var(--primary-light) !important;
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 6px 18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35);
}

.legend-line {
  width: 14px;
  height: 3.5px;
  display: inline-block;
  border-radius: 2px;
}

@keyframes slideUp {
  from { transform: translateY(50px); opacity: 0; }
  to { transform: translateY(0); opacity: 1; }
}

/* =========================================================
   === 3D LANDSLIDE SIMULATION COCKPIT (REFERENCE MATCH) ===
========================================================= */

.sim-cockpit-root {
  background: #080c10;
  color: #e2e8f0;
  font-family: 'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  height: calc(100vh - 35px);
  min-height: 780px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid #1e293b;
  box-shadow: 0 25px 60px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7);
  position: relative;
  margin: -10px -12px;
}

/* --- SIM TOPBAR --- */
.sim-topbar {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 10px 18px;
  background: #0d131a;
  border-bottom: 1px solid #1e293b;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  z-index: 20;
}

.sim-brand-block {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
}

.sim-brand-logo {
  width: 34px;
  height: 34px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #1e3a8a 0%, #0284c7 100%);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(2, 132, 199, 0.35);
}

.sim-logo-svg {
  width: 20px;
  height: 20px;
  fill: #ffffff;
}

.sim-title-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
}

.sim-main-title {
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: -0.2px;
  color: #f8fafc;
  margin: 0;
}

.sim-region-dropdown {
  background: #1e293b;
  color: #38bdf8;
  border: 1px solid #334155;
  border-radius: 6px;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 600;
  padding: 3px 8px;
  outline: none;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.sim-region-dropdown:hover {
  border-color: #38bdf8;
}

.sim-sub-text {
  font-size: 11px;
  color: #64748b;
  margin-top: 1px;
}

.sim-top-right {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 16px;
}

.sim-time-badge {
  font-size: 12px;
  color: #94a3b8;
  font-weight: 500;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
}

.sim-live-indicator {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 7px;
  padding: 4px 10px;
  background: rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.12);
  border: 1px solid rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.28);
  border-radius: 20px;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: #4ade80;
}

.pulse-dot {
  width: 7px;
  height: 7px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: #22c55e;
  box-shadow: 0 0 8px #22c55e;
  animation: pulseGreen 2s infinite ease-in-out;
}

@keyframes pulseGreen {
  0%, 100% { opacity: 1; transform: scale(1); }
  50% { opacity: 0.4; transform: scale(0.85); }
}

.sim-icon-btn {
  position: relative;
  background: #1e293b;
  border: 1px solid #334155;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  width: 32px;
  height: 32px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  transition: all 0.2s;
}

.sim-icon-btn svg {
  width: 16px;
  height: 16px;
  fill: currentColor;
}

.sim-icon-btn:hover {
  background: #334155;
  color: #fff;
}

.sim-bell-badge {
  position: absolute;
  top: -3px;
  right: -3px;
  background: #ef4444;
  color: #fff;
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 700;
  width: 15px;
  height: 15px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  border: 1px solid #0d131a;
}

.sim-user-profile {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 7px;
  background: #131d27;
  padding: 4px 10px 4px 6px;
  border-radius: 20px;
  border: 1px solid #1e293b;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.sim-avatar {
  width: 24px;
  height: 24px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: #334155;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
}

.sim-avatar svg {
  width: 14px;
  height: 14px;
  fill: #94a3b8;
}

.sim-username {
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: #cbd5e1;
}

.sim-user-caret {
  font-size: 9px;
  color: #64748b;
}

/* --- MAIN WORKSPACE GRID --- */
.sim-main-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 235px 1fr 325px;
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: #080c10;
}

/* --- LEFT SIDEBAR (LAYERS) --- */
.sim-layers-sidebar {
  background: #0d131a;
  border-right: 1px solid #1e293b;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  overflow: hidden;
  z-index: 10;
  transition: all 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
}

.sim-layers-sidebar.collapsed {
  width: 0;
  min-width: 0;
  padding: 0;
  border-right: none;
  visibility: hidden;
}

.layers-header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 12px 14px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid #1e293b;
}

.layers-header-title {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #f1f5f9;
}

.layers-collapse-btn {
  background: transparent;
  border: 0;
  color: #64748b;
  font-size: 13px;
  padding: 2px 6px;
  border-radius: 4px;
  transition: all 0.2s;
}

.layers-collapse-btn:hover {
  background: #1e293b;
  color: #fff;
}

.layers-search-box {
  position: relative;
  padding: 8px 12px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid #1e293b;
}

.layers-search-icon {
  position: absolute;
  left: 20px;
  top: 16px;
  width: 14px;
  height: 14px;
  fill: #64748b;
}

.layers-search-box input {
  width: 100%;
  background: #131d27;
  border: 1px solid #1e293b;
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 6px 10px 6px 28px;
  font-size: 11px;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  outline: none;
}

.layers-search-box input:focus {
  border-color: #38bdf8;
}

.layers-scroll-content {
  flex: 1;
  overflow-y: auto;
  padding: 6px 0;
}

.layer-group {
  margin-bottom: 2px;
}

.layer-group-header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 7px 14px;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #94a3b8;
  cursor: pointer;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.02);
  user-select: none;
}

.group-caret {
  font-size: 10px;
  color: #64748b;
  transition: transform 0.2s;
}

.layer-group-body {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  padding: 3px 0 6px;
}

.layer-item {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  padding: 5px 14px 5px 22px;
  font-size: 11px;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: background 0.15s;
}

.layer-item:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04);
  color: #fff;
}

.layer-item input[type="checkbox"] {
  accent-color: #0284c7;
  width: 13px;
  height: 13px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.layer-icon {
  font-size: 12px;
  width: 14px;
  text-align: center;
}

.layer-name {
  flex: 1;
  white-space: nowrap;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
}

.layer-sub-caret {
  color: #64748b;
  font-size: 12px;
}

/* Map tools in left panel */
.layers-map-tools {
  padding: 10px 12px;
  background: #090e13;
  border-top: 1px solid #1e293b;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.map-tools-title {
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 700;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.8px;
  color: #64748b;
  margin-bottom: 6px;
}

.map-tools-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  gap: 4px;
}

.tool-btn {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 5px 2px;
  background: #131d27;
  border: 1px solid #1e293b;
  border-radius: 6px;
  color: #94a3b8;
  font-size: 9px;
  gap: 2px;
  transition: all 0.2s;
}

.tool-btn:hover {
  background: #1e293b;
  color: #38bdf8;
  border-color: #38bdf8;
}

.tool-icon {
  font-size: 12px;
}

/* --- CENTER: 3D VIEWPORT & TIMELINE --- */
.sim-center-column {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-width: 0;
  position: relative;
  background: #05080c;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.sim-viewport-wrapper {
  flex: 1;
  position: relative;
  min-height: 0;
  background: #05080c;
}

.sim-map-canvas {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
}

/* 2D / 3D / Terrain Switcher Pills */
.sim-viewmode-pills {
  position: absolute;
  top: 14px;
  left: 16px;
  z-index: 10;
  display: flex;
  background: rgba(13, 19, 26, 0.85);
  backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 3px;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);
}

.viewmode-btn {
  padding: 5px 12px;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 700;
  border-radius: 6px;
  border: 0;
  background: transparent;
  color: #94a3b8;
  transition: all 0.2s;
}

.viewmode-btn:hover {
  color: #fff;
}

.viewmode-btn.active {
  background: #0284c7;
  color: #ffffff;
  box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(2, 132, 199, 0.4);
}

/* 3D Risk Heatmap Pill Button */
.viewmode-heatmap-btn {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 5px;
  background: rgba(234, 88, 12, 0.16);
  color: #fdba74;
  border: 1px solid rgba(234, 88, 12, 0.32);
}

.viewmode-heatmap-btn:hover {
  background: rgba(234, 88, 12, 0.3);
  color: #fff;
  border-color: #f97316;
}

.viewmode-btn.active.viewmode-heatmap-btn {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ea580c, #dc2626) !important;
  color: #ffffff !important;
  box-shadow: 0 2px 14px rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.6) !important;
  border-color: #f87171 !important;
}

/* Hypsometric Risk Colormap Legend */
.sim-heatmap-legend {
  position: absolute;
  bottom: 48px;
  right: 16px;
  z-index: 12;
  background: rgba(13, 19, 26, 0.9);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 8px 12px;
  box-shadow: 0 6px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 6px;
  min-width: 220px;
  pointer-events: none;
  transition: opacity 0.3s ease, transform 0.3s ease;
}

.sim-heatmap-legend.hidden {
  opacity: 0;
  transform: translateY(10px);
  pointer-events: none;
}

.heatmap-legend-title {
  font-size: 9.5px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.8px;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: #f1f5f9;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 14px;
}

.heatmap-legend-bar {
  width: 100%;
  height: 10px;
  border-radius: 4px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #2e1065 0%, #1e3a8a 18%, #0284c7 35%, #06b6d4 50%, #10b981 65%, #eab308 82%, #dc2626 100%);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.25);
  box-shadow: 0 2px 6px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
}

.heatmap-legend-labels {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  font-size: 8px;
  font-family: monospace;
  color: #94a3b8;
  font-weight: 600;
}

/* 3D Coordinate Collar Axis Grid (Matching reference image) */
.sim-coord-collar {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  z-index: 9;
  transition: opacity 0.3s ease;
}

.sim-coord-collar.hidden {
  opacity: 0;
}

.collar-axis-lat {
  position: absolute;
  left: 14px;
  top: 70px;
  bottom: 80px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  justify-content: space-between;
  font-size: 9px;
  font-family: monospace;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.85);
  text-shadow: 0 1px 4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9), 0 0 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8);
  border-left: 2px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.5);
  padding-left: 6px;
}

.collar-axis-lon {
  position: absolute;
  bottom: 30px;
  left: 50px;
  right: 250px;
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  font-size: 9px;
  font-family: monospace;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.85);
  text-shadow: 0 1px 4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9), 0 0 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8);
  border-bottom: 2px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.5);
  padding-bottom: 4px;
}

.collar-tick {
  white-space: nowrap;
}

/* Compass Widget */
.sim-compass-widget {
  position: absolute;
  top: 14px;
  right: 16px;
  z-index: 10;
  width: 36px;
  height: 36px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: rgba(13, 19, 26, 0.85);
  backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);
}

.compass-circle {
  position: relative;
  width: 24px;
  height: 24px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
}

.compass-needle {
  position: absolute;
  top: 3px;
  width: 0;
  height: 0;
  border-left: 4px solid transparent;
  border-right: 4px solid transparent;
  border-bottom: 10px solid #ef4444;
}

.compass-label {
  position: absolute;
  bottom: -1px;
  font-size: 8px;
  font-weight: 900;
  color: #ffffff;
}

/* Scale Bar */
.sim-scalebar {
  position: absolute;
  bottom: 12px;
  left: 16px;
  z-index: 10;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 600;
  background: rgba(13, 19, 26, 0.8);
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  padding: 4px 8px;
  border-radius: 4px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
}

.scale-ticks {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 14px;
  margin-bottom: 2px;
}

.scale-line {
  height: 3px;
  background: #ffffff;
  border-radius: 2px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8);
}

/* Coordinate HUD */
.sim-coord-hud {
  position: absolute;
  bottom: 12px;
  right: 16px;
  z-index: 10;
  background: rgba(13, 19, 26, 0.85);
  backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
  padding: 5px 12px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  font-size: 10px;
  color: #94a3b8;
  font-family: monospace;
}

.coord-val {
  color: #38bdf8;
  font-weight: 700;
}

/* --- SPATIAL BADGES ON 3D MAP --- */
.spatial-badge {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  background: rgba(13, 19, 26, 0.92);
  backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.18);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 3px 8px 3px 4px;
  box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);
  pointer-events: none;
  cursor: crosshair;
  white-space: nowrap;
  transition: transform 0.2s ease;
}

.spatial-badge:hover {
  transform: scale(1.08) translateY(-2px);
  border-color: #38bdf8;
}

.badge-icon-box {
  width: 22px;
  height: 22px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  margin-right: 6px;
}

.badge-content {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.badge-title {
  font-size: 10px;
  color: #f8fafc;
  line-height: 1.2;
}

.badge-sub {
  font-size: 9px;
  line-height: 1.1;
}

.badge-forest {
  padding: 3px 7px;
  gap: 5px;
  background: rgba(6, 78, 59, 0.85);
  border-color: #059669;
}

.badge-village {
  padding: 3px 8px;
  gap: 6px;
}

.badge-icon-circle {
  width: 8px;
  height: 8px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  display: inline-block;
}

.badge-label-text {
  font-size: 10px;
}

.terrain-label {
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #e2e8f0;
  text-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.95), 0 0 3px #000;
  pointer-events: none;
  letter-spacing: 0.3px;
}

.label-road { color: #fde047; }
.label-river { color: #38bdf8; }
.label-valley { color: #cbd5e1; font-style: italic; }

.contour-elev-label {
  font-size: 9px;
  font-family: monospace;
  color: rgba(226, 232, 240, 0.7);
  text-shadow: 0 1px 4px #000;
  pointer-events: none;
}

/* --- BOTTOM TIMELINE PANEL --- */
.sim-timeline-panel {
  background: #0d131a;
  border-top: 1px solid #1e293b;
  padding: 10px 18px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 8px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  z-index: 15;
  min-width: 0;
}

.timeline-controls-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 14px;
  min-width: 0;
  flex-wrap: nowrap;
}

.timeline-playback-btns {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 5px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.t-btn {
  background: #1e293b;
  border: 1px solid #334155;
  border-radius: 6px;
  width: 30px;
  height: 30px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  transition: all 0.2s;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.t-btn:hover {
  background: #334155;
  color: #fff;
}

.play-btn {
  background: #0284c7;
  border-color: #0284c7;
  width: 34px;
  height: 34px;
  border-radius: 7px;
}

.play-btn:hover {
  background: #0369a1;
}

.timeline-slider-container {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 260px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 4px;
  padding: 0 4px;
}

.timeline-range-slider {
  width: 100%;
  accent-color: #0284c7;
  height: 6px;
  background: #1e293b;
  border-radius: 3px;
  outline: none;
  cursor: pointer;
  margin: 0;
}

.timeline-ticks-labels {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  width: 100%;
  padding: 0 2px;
}

.tick-lbl {
  font-size: 10px;
  color: #64748b;
  font-family: 'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;
  font-weight: 500;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: color 0.15s, transform 0.15s;
  white-space: nowrap;
  user-select: none;
}

.tick-lbl:hover {
  color: #cbd5e1;
}

.tick-lbl.active {
  color: #38bdf8;
  font-weight: 700;
  transform: scale(1.08);
}

.timeline-speed-box {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.t-speed-dropdown {
  background: #1e293b;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  border: 1px solid #334155;
  border-radius: 6px;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 600;
  padding: 4px 6px;
  outline: none;
}

.t-current-tag {
  background: #131d27;
  border: 1px solid #1e293b;
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 4px 8px;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: #38bdf8;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

/* Filmstrip & Anim Controls */
.timeline-bottom-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 14px;
  min-width: 0;
  flex-wrap: nowrap;
}

.timeline-filmstrip {
  display: flex;
  gap: 8px;
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  overflow-x: auto;
  scrollbar-width: thin;
  padding-bottom: 2px;
}

.filmstrip-card {
  position: relative;
  border: 2px solid #1e293b;
  border-radius: 6px;
  overflow: hidden;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all 0.2s ease;
  flex: 0 0 74px;
  height: 46px;
  background: #000;
}

.filmstrip-card:hover {
  border-color: #38bdf8;
  transform: translateY(-2px);
}

.filmstrip-card.active {
  border-color: #0284c7;
  box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(2, 132, 199, 0.6);
}

.thumb-canvas {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  display: block;
}

.thumb-time {
  position: absolute;
  bottom: 2px;
  right: 3px;
  font-size: 8px;
  font-family: monospace;
  color: #fff;
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8);
  padding: 1px 3px;
  border-radius: 3px;
}

.timeline-anim-controls {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  background: #131d27;
  border: 1px solid #1e293b;
  padding: 6px 10px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.anim-ctrl-title {
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #94a3b8;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.anim-ctrl-checkboxes {
  display: flex;
  gap: 8px;
}

.anim-chk-item {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 5px;
  font-size: 10px;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.anim-chk-item input {
  accent-color: #0284c7;
  width: 12px;
  height: 12px;
}

.anim-replay-btn {
  background: #1e293b;
  border: 1px solid #334155;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 4px 8px;
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 600;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  transition: all 0.2s;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.anim-replay-btn:hover {
  background: #334155;
  color: #fff;
}

/* --- RIGHT PANEL: ANALYTICS --- */
.sim-analytics-sidebar {
  background: #0d131a;
  border-left: 1px solid #1e293b;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 12px 14px;
  overflow-y: auto;
  z-index: 10;
}

.analytics-card {
  background: #131d27;
  border: 1px solid #1e293b;
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 12px 14px;
}

.card-heading {
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #f1f5f9;
  letter-spacing: -0.1px;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
}

/* 1. Risk Overview */
.risk-overview-content {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 12px;
}

.risk-radial-box {
  position: relative;
  width: 90px;
  height: 90px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.risk-donut-svg {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  transform: rotate(-90deg);
}

.donut-bg {
  fill: none;
  stroke: #1e293b;
  stroke-width: 9;
}

.donut-fg {
  fill: none;
  stroke: #ef4444;
  stroke-width: 9;
  stroke-linecap: round;
  stroke-dasharray: 314.15;
  transition: stroke-dashoffset 0.5s ease, stroke 0.5s ease;
}

.donut-center-text {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  text-align: center;
}

/* Real-ML provenance badge under the risk donut (Option A/B wiring) */
.donut-ml-source {
  position: absolute;
  left: 50%;
  bottom: -18px;
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  white-space: nowrap;
  font-size: 9.5px;
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  color: #94a3b8;
  background: rgba(30, 41, 59, 0.75);
  border: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.25);
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 2px 8px;
  pointer-events: auto;
}

.donut-val {
  font-size: 20px;
  font-weight: 800;
  line-height: 1;
  color: #f8fafc;
}

.donut-level {
  font-size: 9px;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
  margin-top: 2px;
}

.risk-scale-legend {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 4px;
  flex: 1;
}

.legend-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  font-size: 10px;
  color: #94a3b8;
}

.legend-dot {
  width: 7px;
  height: 7px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  margin-right: 6px;
}

.legend-name { flex: 1; }
.legend-range { font-family: monospace; color: #64748b; font-size: 9px; }

/* 2. Conditions Grid */
.conditions-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
  gap: 8px;
}

.cond-card {
  background: #090e13;
  border: 1px solid #1e293b;
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 8px 10px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
}

.cond-icon {
  font-size: 16px;
}

.cond-info {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.cond-lbl {
  font-size: 9px;
  color: #64748b;
  line-height: 1.1;
}

.cond-val {
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #f1f5f9;
  line-height: 1.3;
}

/* 3. Progression Chart Box */
.progression-chart-box {
  width: 100%;
  height: 110px;
}

/* 4. Scenario Comparison */
.scenario-tabs {
  display: flex;
  gap: 4px;
  margin-bottom: 8px;
  background: #090e13;
  padding: 3px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  border: 1px solid #1e293b;
}

.scen-tab {
  flex: 1;
  padding: 4px 6px;
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 600;
  border-radius: 4px;
  border: 0;
  background: transparent;
  color: #94a3b8;
  transition: all 0.2s;
}

.scen-tab.active {
  background: #0284c7;
  color: #ffffff;
}

.scenario-table-box {
  overflow-x: auto;
}

.scenario-table {
  width: 100%;
  border-collapse: collapse;
  font-size: 10px;
}

.scenario-table th {
  text-align: left;
  padding: 5px 6px;
  font-size: 8px;
  font-weight: 700;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: #64748b;
  border-bottom: 1px solid #1e293b;
}

.scenario-table td {
  padding: 5px 6px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04);
}

.scen-prob-badge {
  padding: 2px 6px;
  border-radius: 4px;
  font-weight: 700;
  font-size: 9px;
}

.risk-moderate { background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.15); color: #fbbf24; border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3); }
.risk-high { background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.18); color: #f87171; border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.35); }
.risk-extreme { background: rgba(185, 28, 28, 0.25); color: #fca5a5; border: 1px solid rgba(185, 28, 28, 0.5); }

/* 5. Infrastructure KPIs */
.infra-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
  gap: 8px;
}

.infra-item {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  background: #090e13;
  border: 1px solid #1e293b;
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 8px 10px;
}

.infra-icon {
  font-size: 16px;
}

.infra-details {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.infra-name {
  font-size: 9px;
  color: #94a3b8;
}

.infra-nums strong {
  font-size: 13px;
  color: #f8fafc;
}

.infra-nums small {
  font-size: 8px;
}

/* --- BOTTOM STATUS & ALERT BAR --- */
.sim-bottom-bar {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 8px 18px;
  background: #090e13;
  border-top: 1px solid #1e293b;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  z-index: 20;
}

.status-online-tag {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  font-size: 11px;
}

.active-threat-pill {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
}

.threat-alert-box {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  background: rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.12);
  border: 1px solid rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.25);
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 4px 10px;
}

.view-details-btn {
  background: #1e293b;
  border: 1px solid #334155;
  color: #f8fafc;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 600;
  padding: 4px 12px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  transition: all 0.2s;
}

.view-details-btn:hover {
  background: #0284c7;
  border-color: #0284c7;
}

/* --- MODAL DIALOG --- */
.sim-modal-backdrop {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.75);
  backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
  z-index: 100;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 20px;
  animation: fadeIn 0.2s ease;
}

@keyframes fadeIn {
  from { opacity: 0; }
  to { opacity: 1; }
}

.sim-modal-card {
  width: 580px;
  max-width: 95%;
  background: #0d131a;
  border: 1px solid #334155;
  border-radius: 12px;
  box-shadow: 0 25px 60px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8);
  overflow: hidden;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.sim-modal-header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 14px 18px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid #1e293b;
}

.sim-modal-close {
  background: transparent;
  border: 0;
  color: #94a3b8;
  font-size: 16px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.sim-modal-close:hover {
  color: #fff;
}

.sim-modal-body {
  padding: 16px 18px;
  overflow-y: auto;
  max-height: 480px;
}

.modal-summary-badge {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  gap: 10px;
  background: #131d27;
  border: 1px solid #1e293b;
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 16px;
}

.modal-section-title {
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #f1f5f9;
  margin-bottom: 8px;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.evac-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  gap: 8px;
}

.evac-stat-box {
  background: #090e13;
  border: 1px solid #1e293b;
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 10px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 3px;
}

.stat-lbl {
  font-size: 9px;
  color: #64748b;
}

.stat-val {
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.incident-action-list {
  padding-left: 18px;
  font-size: 11px;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  line-height: 1.6;
}

.incident-action-list li {
  margin-bottom: 6px;
}

.sim-modal-footer {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: flex-end;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 12px 18px;
  background: #090e13;
  border-top: 1px solid #1e293b;
}

.modal-export-btn {
  background: #0284c7;
  color: #fff;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 6px 14px;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 600;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.modal-export-btn:hover {
  background: #0369a1;
}

.modal-dismiss-btn {
  background: #1e293b;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  border: 1px solid #334155;
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 6px 14px;
  font-size: 11px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.modal-dismiss-btn:hover {
  background: #334155;
  color: #fff;
}

.nav-badge-live {
  background: #ef4444;
  color: #fff;
  font-size: 8px;
  font-weight: 800;
  padding: 2px 5px;
  border-radius: 4px;
  margin-left: auto;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}


/* =========================================================
   LANDSLIDE EARLY WARNING & SIMULATION SYSTEM STYLES
   Pixel-accurate dark glassmorphism military/geotechnical command center
   ========================================================= */

.landslide-app-shell {
  min-height: 100vh;
  background-color: #070e0a;
  background-image: radial-gradient(circle at 50% 0%, #0d1a13 0%, #070e0a 75%);
  color: #f1f5f9;
  font-family: 'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

/* TOPBAR */
.ls-topbar {
  height: 60px;
  padding: 0 20px;
  background: rgba(11, 18, 14, 0.95);
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  z-index: 100;
  backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
}

.ls-brand {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
}

.ls-brand-icon {
  width: 34px;
  height: 34px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #1e3a8a, #0284c7);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  color: #fff;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 12px rgba(2, 132, 199, 0.35);
}

.ls-title {
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.2px;
  color: #ffffff;
  margin: 0;
}

.ls-subtitle {
  font-size: 10px;
  color: #94a3b8;
  font-weight: 500;
  letter-spacing: 0.2px;
}

.ls-top-actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
}

.ls-region-selector-wrapper {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12);
  border-radius: 7px;
  padding: 4px 10px;
}

.ls-pin-icon {
  font-size: 13px;
}

.ls-region-select {
  background: transparent;
  border: none;
  color: #e2e8f0;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 700;
  outline: none;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.ls-region-select option {
  background: #0f172a;
  color: #fff;
}

.ls-time-badge {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 7px;
  padding: 6px 12px;
  font-size: 11px;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ls-alert-pill {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 6px 14px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
  color: #fff;
  cursor: default;
}

.ls-alert-pill.risk-red {
  background: #dc2626;
  box-shadow: 0 0 14px rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.55);
}

.ls-alert-pill.risk-yellow {
  background: #d97706;
  box-shadow: 0 0 14px rgba(217, 119, 6, 0.55);
}

.ls-alert-pill.risk-green {
  background: #16a34a;
  box-shadow: 0 0 14px rgba(22, 163, 74, 0.55);
}

.ls-alert-pill.risk-black {
  background: #09090b;
  border: 1px solid #ef4444;
  box-shadow: 0 0 16px rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.8);
}

/* LAYOUT BODY */
.ls-layout-body {
  display: flex;
  flex: 1;
  min-height: calc(100vh - 92px);
}

/* SIDEBAR */
.ls-sidebar {
  width: 175px;
  background: rgba(10, 16, 12, 0.9);
  border-right: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.07);
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 14px 10px;
}

.ls-nav {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 4px;
}

.ls-nav-btn {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 9px 12px;
  border: none;
  background: transparent;
  color: #94a3b8;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 600;
  border-radius: 8px;
  cursor: pointer;
  text-align: left;
  transition: all 0.2s ease;
}

.ls-nav-btn:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  color: #ffffff;
}

.ls-nav-btn.active {
  background: rgba(2, 132, 199, 0.2);
  color: #38bdf8;
  font-weight: 700;
  border-left: 3px solid #0284c7;
}

.ls-sidebar-footer {
  padding-top: 12px;
  border-top: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
}

.ls-sidebar-status {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  font-size: 10px;
  color: #94a3b8;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ls-pulse-dot {
  width: 7px;
  height: 7px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: #22c55e;
  box-shadow: 0 0 8px #22c55e;
}

/* MAIN CONTENT */
.ls-main {
  flex: 1;
  padding: 16px 20px;
  overflow-y: auto;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 16px;
}

/* TOP KPI CARDS */
.ls-kpi-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
  gap: 14px;
}

.ls-kpi-card {
  background: rgba(14, 22, 17, 0.85);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 12px 14px;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25);
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  justify-content: space-between;
}

.ls-kpi-card.warning-accent {
  border-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3);
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.08) 0%, rgba(14, 22, 17, 0.95) 100%);
}

.ls-kpi-header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
}

.ls-kpi-icon-wrap {
  width: 36px;
  height: 36px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  font-size: 18px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
}

.ls-kpi-icon-wrap.water-drop { color: #38bdf8; }
.ls-kpi-icon-wrap.rain-cloud { color: #60a5fa; }
.ls-kpi-icon-wrap.seismic-wave { color: #f59e0b; }
.ls-kpi-icon-wrap.danger-triangle { color: #ef4444; }

.ls-kpi-title {
  font-size: 11px;
  color: #94a3b8;
  font-weight: 600;
  margin-bottom: 2px;
}

.ls-kpi-val-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: baseline;
  gap: 8px;
}

.ls-kpi-val {
  font-size: 18px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.3px;
  color: #ffffff;
}

.ls-kpi-val.text-red { color: #ef4444; }

.ls-kpi-trend {
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 700;
}
.ls-kpi-trend.up { color: #38bdf8; }

.ls-kpi-badge {
  font-size: 10px;
  color: #94a3b8;
  font-weight: 600;
}
.ls-kpi-badge.text-gold { color: #f59e0b; }

.ls-kpi-ci {
  font-size: 10px;
  color: #94a3b8;
}

.ls-sparkline-box {
  margin-top: 8px;
  height: 36px;
}

.ls-sparkline-box canvas {
  width: 100% !important;
  height: 36px !important;
}

.ls-prob-meter-box {
  margin-top: 14px;
}

.ls-prob-bar-track {
  width: 100%;
  height: 7px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 4px;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.ls-prob-bar-fill {
  height: 100%;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #f59e0b, #ef4444);
  border-radius: 4px;
  transition: width 0.5s ease;
}

/* MIDDLE SECTION */
.ls-middle-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1.85fr) minmax(320px, 1.15fr);
  gap: 16px;
}

.ls-center-sim-panel {
  height: 540px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  box-shadow: 0 8px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);
}

.ls-right-column {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 14px;
}

/* PANELS */
.ls-panel {
  background: rgba(14, 22, 17, 0.88);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 12px 14px;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25);
}

.ls-panel-header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
}

.ls-panel-title {
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
  color: #ffffff;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.ls-panel-title small {
  font-size: 10px;
  color: #94a3b8;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: none;
}

.ls-live-tag {
  font-size: 10px;
  color: #22c55e;
  font-weight: 700;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 5px;
}

.ls-dot-green {
  width: 6px;
  height: 6px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: #22c55e;
  box-shadow: 0 0 6px #22c55e;
}

/* TELEMETRY TABLE */
.ls-telemetry-table-wrapper {
  overflow-x: auto;
}

.ls-telemetry-table {
  width: 100%;
  border-collapse: collapse;
  font-size: 11px;
}

.ls-telemetry-table th {
  text-align: left;
  color: #64748b;
  font-size: 9px;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  padding: 4px 6px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
}

.ls-telemetry-table td {
  padding: 5px 6px;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04);
}

.ls-telemetry-table td strong {
  color: #ffffff;
}

.trend-up { color: #ef4444; font-weight: 800; }
.trend-flat { color: #64748b; font-weight: 800; }

/* ALERT LEVEL CARD */
.ls-alert-level-card {
  border-left: 3px solid #ef4444;
}

.ls-alert-level-header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
}

.ls-kicker {
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.8px;
  color: #94a3b8;
}

.ls-alert-badge-lg {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  padding: 4px 10px;
  border-radius: 6px;
}

.ls-alert-badge-lg.risk-red {
  background: rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.2);
  border: 1px solid #ef4444;
  color: #ef4444;
}

.ls-level-title {
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 900;
}

.ls-level-sub {
  font-size: 9px;
  color: #cbd5e1;
}

.ls-alert-scale-bar {
  display: flex;
  gap: 4px;
  height: 6px;
  margin-bottom: 12px;
}

.ls-alert-scale-bar .step {
  flex: 1;
  border-radius: 3px;
  opacity: 0.25;
}

.step-green { background: #22c55e; }
.step-yellow { background: #eab308; }
.step-red { background: #ef4444; }
.step-black { background: #991b1b; }

.ls-alert-scale-bar .step.active {
  opacity: 1;
  box-shadow: 0 0 6px currentColor;
}

.ls-rec-actions-box {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 8px 10px;
}

.ls-rec-title {
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 800;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  margin-bottom: 4px;
}

.ls-rec-list {
  list-style: none;
  padding: 0;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 10px;
  color: #94a3b8;
  line-height: 1.4;
}

/* CHART BOX */
.ls-chart-legend {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  font-size: 10px;
}

.lg-cur { color: #ef4444; }
.lg-peak { color: #94a3b8; }
.lg-trend { color: #ef4444; font-weight: 700; }

.ls-chart-box {
  width: 100%;
  height: 125px;
}

.ls-chart-box canvas {
  width: 100% !important;
  height: 125px !important;
}

/* SCENARIO COMPARISON */
.ls-sc-tabs {
  display: flex;
  gap: 4px;
}

.ls-sc-btn {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
  border-radius: 6px;
  color: #94a3b8;
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 700;
  padding: 4px 8px;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all 0.2s;
}

.ls-sc-btn:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  color: #fff;
}

.ls-sc-btn.active {
  background: #0284c7;
  border-color: #0284c7;
  color: #ffffff;
}

.ls-sc-table-wrapper {
  overflow-x: auto;
}

.ls-sc-table {
  width: 100%;
  border-collapse: collapse;
  font-size: 10px;
}

.ls-sc-table th {
  text-align: left;
  color: #64748b;
  padding: 4px 6px;
  font-size: 9px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
}

.ls-sc-table td {
  padding: 5px 6px;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04);
}

.sc-prob-tag {
  padding: 2px 6px;
  border-radius: 4px;
  font-weight: 800;
  font-size: 9px;
}

.tag-red { background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.2); color: #ef4444; border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.4); }
.tag-yellow { background: rgba(234, 179, 8, 0.2); color: #eab308; border: 1px solid rgba(234, 179, 8, 0.4); }
.tag-green { background: rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.2); color: #22c55e; border: 1px solid rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.4); }

/* BOTTOM GRID (4 PANELS) */
.ls-bottom-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
  gap: 14px;
}

/* INFRASTRUCTURE */
.ls-infra-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  gap: 10px;
}

.ls-infra-item {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 8px;
  text-align: center;
}

.ls-infra-icon {
  font-size: 16px;
  margin-bottom: 2px;
}

.ls-infra-count {
  font-size: 16px;
  font-weight: 800;
  color: #fff;
}

.ls-infra-label {
  font-size: 9px;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ls-infra-sub {
  font-size: 8px;
  color: #64748b;
}

/* EVACUATION */
.ls-evac-container {
  display: flex;
  gap: 12px;
  align-items: center;
}

.ls-evac-minimap {
  width: 140px;
  height: 110px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  background: #0f1712;
}

.ls-evac-details {
  flex: 1;
}

.ls-evac-legend {
  font-size: 9px;
  color: #94a3b8;
  margin-bottom: 8px;
}

.ls-el-item {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 5px;
  margin-bottom: 3px;
}

.box-red { width: 8px; height: 8px; background: #ef4444; border-radius: 2px; }
.box-yellow { width: 8px; height: 8px; background: #eab308; border-radius: 2px; }
.box-green { width: 8px; height: 8px; background: #22c55e; border-radius: 2px; }

.ls-evac-kpi .ev-lbl {
  font-size: 9px;
  color: #64748b;
}

.ls-evac-kpi .ev-val {
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 800;
  color: #fff;
  margin-bottom: 4px;
}

/* RECENT ALERTS */
.ls-events-list {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 6px;
}

.ls-event-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  font-size: 10px;
  padding: 4px 6px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.02);
}

.ev-time {
  font-size: 9px;
  color: #64748b;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ev-badge-red { color: #ef4444; }
.ev-badge-yellow { color: #eab308; }
.ev-badge-info { color: #38bdf8; }
.ev-badge-warn { color: #f97316; }

.ev-desc {
  flex: 1;
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
}

.tag-info { background: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.2); color: #38bdf8; padding: 1px 4px; border-radius: 3px; font-size: 8px; }
.tag-warn { background: rgba(249, 115, 22, 0.2); color: #f97316; padding: 1px 4px; border-radius: 3px; font-size: 8px; }

/* GENERATED REPORT CARD */
.ls-btn-pdf {
  background: #0284c7;
  border: none;
  color: #fff;
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 700;
  padding: 4px 8px;
  border-radius: 5px;
  cursor: pointer;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4px;
}

.ls-btn-pdf:hover {
  background: #0369a1;
}

.ls-report-card-body {
  font-size: 10px;
}

.rep-title {
  font-weight: 800;
  color: #fff;
  font-size: 11px;
}

.rep-date {
  font-size: 9px;
  color: #64748b;
  margin-bottom: 6px;
}

.rep-meta-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 4px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.02);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  padding: 6px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  margin-bottom: 6px;
}

.rm-row {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  font-size: 9px;
}

.rm-row span { color: #64748b; }
.rm-row strong { color: #cbd5e1; }

.rep-actions-mini {
  font-size: 9px;
  color: #94a3b8;
}

.ra-lbl {
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  margin-bottom: 2px;
}

.ra-item {
  line-height: 1.3;
}

/* BOTTOM STATUS BAR */
.ls-bottom-bar {
  height: 32px;
  padding: 0 20px;
  background: rgba(9, 14, 11, 0.96);
  border-top: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  font-size: 10px;
  color: #64748b;
}

.ls-bb-left {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
}

.ls-bb-left strong {
  color: #22c55e;
}

.ls-bb-sep {
  color: #475569;
}

.ls-bb-mid {
  color: #94a3b8;
}

.ls-bb-right {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  color: #64748b;
}

/* 3D SIMULATOR VIEW TABS */
.sim-tab-btn {
  background: rgba(18, 26, 20, 0.85);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12);
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 5px 9px;
  font-size: 10px;
  color: #cbd5e1;
  font-weight: 700;
  cursor: pointer;
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  transition: all 0.2s;
}

.sim-tab-btn:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15);
  color: #ffffff;
}

.sim-tab-btn.active {
  background: #0284c7;
  border-color: #38bdf8;
  color: #ffffff;
}

/* 3D Click-to-Heatmap Ping Ripple Marker */
.sim-click-ping-ring {
  width: 14px;
  height: 14px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: #ff2a4b;
  box-shadow: 0 0 15px #ff2a4b;
  pointer-events: none;
  animation: pingRipple 1.2s cubic-bezier(0, 0.2, 0.8, 1) forwards;
}

@keyframes pingRipple {
  0% {
    transform: scale(0.6);
    opacity: 1;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 0 rgba(255, 42, 75, 0.9);
  }
  60% {
    transform: scale(3.5);
    opacity: 0.7;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 18px rgba(255, 42, 75, 0.0);
  }
  100% {
    transform: scale(5.0);
    opacity: 0;
  }
}


/* =========================================================
   DISASTER VIDEO STUDIO (vstudio)
========================================================= */

.vstudio {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 20px;
  padding-bottom: 30px;
}

.vstudio-hero {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 24px;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.vstudio-kicker {
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: 1.6px;
  color: var(--primary-light);
  margin-bottom: 6px;
}

.vstudio-hero h2 {
  font-size: 22px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.3px;
}

.vstudio-hero p {
  margin-top: 8px;
  max-width: 560px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 12px;
  line-height: 1.6;
}

.vstudio-stage-rail {
  display: flex;
  gap: 8px;
}

.vs-stage {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 7px;
  padding: 8px 12px;
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: var(--surface);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.8px;
  opacity: 0.65;
  transition: 0.2s ease;
}

.vs-stage span {
  width: 18px;
  height: 18px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--surface-2);
  font-size: 9px;
}

.vs-stage.active {
  opacity: 1;
  color: var(--text);
  border-color: var(--primary-light);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(111, 133, 57, 0.35);
}

.vstudio-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1.35fr) minmax(300px, 1fr);
  gap: 20px;
  align-items: start;
}

.vstudio-panel {
  background: var(--surface);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: var(--radius);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow);
  overflow: hidden;
}

.vstudio-panel-head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 14px;
  padding: 16px 18px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
}

.vstudio-panel-head h3 {
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.vs-draw-modes {
  display: flex;
  gap: 6px;
}

.vs-mode-btn {
  padding: 7px 10px;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 800;
  transition: 0.2s ease;
}

.vs-mode-btn:hover {
  color: var(--text);
  transform: translateY(-1px);
}

.vs-mode-btn.active {
  background: var(--primary);
  border-color: var(--primary);
  color: #fff;
}

.vs-map-wrap {
  position: relative;
  height: 430px;
}

.vs-map {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
}

.vs-map-hint {
  position: absolute;
  top: 12px;
  left: 12px;
  z-index: 5;
  padding: 7px 11px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: rgba(14, 21, 17, 0.88);
  color: #fff;
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 700;
  pointer-events: none;
}

.vs-map-legend {
  position: absolute;
  bottom: 12px;
  right: 12px;
  z-index: 5;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 7px 11px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: rgba(14, 21, 17, 0.88);
  color: #cbd5cf;
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 700;
  pointer-events: none;
}

.vs-map-legend .dot {
  width: 9px;
  height: 9px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  display: inline-block;
  margin-right: 4px;
}

.vs-map-legend .dot.dem { background: #c47a3a; }
.vs-map-legend .dot.sat { background: #3a7ca5; }

.vs-selection-info {
  padding: 14px 18px;
  font-size: 11px;
  color: var(--muted);
  border-top: 1px solid var(--border);
}

.vs-sel-stats {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 6px 16px;
  align-items: center;
}

.vs-sel-stats strong { color: var(--text); font-size: 11px; }
.vs-sel-stats .ok { color: var(--green); font-weight: 800; }
.vs-sel-stats .bad { color: var(--red); font-weight: 800; }

.vs-link {
  border: 0;
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--primary-light);
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 800;
  text-decoration: underline;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.vs-config {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 15px;
  padding: 16px 18px;
}

.vs-cfg-row label {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  margin-bottom: 7px;
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: 1px;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.vs-cfg-row label strong { color: var(--text); font-size: 11px; }

.vs-cfg-row input[type="range"] {
  width: 100%;
  accent-color: var(--primary);
}

.vs-range-scale {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  margin-top: 4px;
  font-size: 8px;
  color: var(--muted);
  opacity: 0.7;
}

.vs-inline {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 14px;
}

.vs-inline select {
  width: 100%;
  padding: 9px 10px;
  border-radius: 9px;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  background: var(--surface-2);
  color: var(--text);
  font-size: 11px;
  outline: none;
}

.vs-seg {
  display: flex;
  gap: 8px;
}

.vs-seg-btn {
  flex: 1;
  padding: 10px;
  border-radius: 10px;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  background: var(--surface-2);
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 800;
  transition: 0.2s ease;
}

.vs-seg-btn.active {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #b34a2f, #8a3018);
  border-color: #d4643f;
  color: #fff;
}

.vs-seg-btn[data-mode="flood"].active {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #2f6db3, #1c4a80);
  border-color: #3f8fd4;
}

.vs-checks { flex-direction: row; gap: 18px; }

.vs-check {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 7px;
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--muted);
  cursor: pointer;
}

.vs-check input { accent-color: var(--primary); }

.vs-generate {
  margin: 4px 18px 8px;
  padding: 14px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--primary-light), var(--primary));
  color: #fff;
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: 1px;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 26px rgba(83, 99, 44, 0.35);
  transition: 0.2s ease;
}

.vs-generate:hover:not(:disabled) {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 14px 32px rgba(83, 99, 44, 0.45);
}

.vs-generate:disabled {
  opacity: 0.45;
  cursor: not-allowed;
}

.vs-generate-note {
  margin: 0 18px 16px;
  font-size: 9px;
  color: var(--muted);
  text-align: center;
}

.vs-cancel {
  padding: 7px 12px;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
  color: var(--red);
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.vs-cancel:hover { background: rgba(199, 74, 69, 0.12); }

.vs-stages {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 12px;
  padding: 16px 18px;
}

.vs-pstage {
  display: flex;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 12px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  opacity: 0.6;
  transition: 0.25s ease;
}

.vs-pstage.active {
  opacity: 1;
  border-color: var(--amber);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(197, 138, 39, 0.3);
}

.vs-pstage.done {
  opacity: 1;
  border-color: rgba(79, 138, 91, 0.55);
}

.vs-pstage.error {
  opacity: 1;
  border-color: var(--red);
}

.vs-ps-icon { font-size: 17px; }

.vs-pstage strong {
  display: block;
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: 0.4px;
}

.vs-pstage em {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 3px;
  font-style: normal;
  font-size: 9px;
  color: var(--muted);
  min-height: 22px;
}

.vs-ps-bar {
  margin-top: 7px;
  height: 5px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  background: var(--border);
  overflow: hidden;
}

.vs-ps-bar span {
  display: block;
  height: 100%;
  width: 0%;
  border-radius: inherit;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--primary), var(--primary-light));
  transition: width 0.25s ease;
}

.vs-pstage.error .vs-ps-bar span { background: var(--red); }

.vs-preview-wrap {
  margin: 4px 18px 14px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: #000;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
}

.vs-preview {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  max-height: 62vh;
  object-fit: contain;
}

/* =========================================================
   INTERACTIVE 3D SIMULATION VIEWER
========================================================= */

.vs-3d-panel {
  margin: 4px 18px 14px;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 12px;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: var(--surface-2);
}

.vs-3d-head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 12px 14px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
  background: var(--surface);
}

.vs-3d-head h4 {
  margin: 2px 0 0;
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.vs-3d-toolbar {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 6px;
}

.vs-3d-btn {
  padding: 6px 10px;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: var(--surface);
  color: var(--text);
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 700;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: 0.15s ease;
}

.vs-3d-btn:hover {
  border-color: var(--primary);
  color: var(--primary);
}

.vs-3d-btn select,
select.vs-3d-btn {
  padding: 6px 6px;
}

.vs-3d-record {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #b3362e, #7d1f1a);
  border-color: #b3362e;
  color: #fff;
}

.vs-3d-record:hover {
  color: #fff;
  filter: brightness(1.12);
}

.vs-3d-record.recording {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #e8382f, #a3231c);
  animation: vs3d-pulse 1.1s ease-in-out infinite;
}

@keyframes vs3d-pulse {
  0%, 100% { box-shadow: 0 0 0 0 rgba(232, 56, 47, 0.55); }
  50% { box-shadow: 0 0 0 6px rgba(232, 56, 47, 0); }
}

.vs-3d-sep {
  width: 1px;
  height: 20px;
  background: var(--border);
  margin: 0 2px;
}

.vs-3d-exag {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.vs-3d-exag input[type='range'] {
  width: 90px;
}

.vs-3d-container {
  position: relative;
  width: 100%;
  height: 62vh;
  min-height: 420px;
  background: #0b0f14;
  cursor: grab;
}

.vs-3d-container:active {
  cursor: grabbing;
}

.vs-3d-container canvas {
  display: block;
}

.vs-3d-hud {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 8px;
  padding: 10px 14px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--border);
  background: var(--surface);
}

.vs-3d-hud .chip {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 5px 10px;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.4px;
  color: var(--muted);
  background: var(--surface-2);
}

.vs-3d-hud .chip strong {
  color: var(--text);
  font-size: 11px;
}

.vs-export-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 16px;
  padding: 0 18px 14px;
}

.vs-download {
  padding: 12px 22px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #2f6db3, #1c4a80);
  color: #fff;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: 0.6px;
  box-shadow: 0 8px 22px rgba(47, 109, 179, 0.4);
  transition: 0.2s ease;
}

.vs-download:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); }

.vs-export-meta {
  font-size: 10px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-weight: 700;
}

.vs-impact-summary {
  margin: 0 18px 18px;
  padding: 15px 17px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
}

.vs-impact-title {
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: 1.2px;
  color: var(--primary-light);
  margin-bottom: 12px;
}

.vs-impact-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(150px, 1fr));
  gap: 10px 22px;
}

.vs-impact-grid > div {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 3px;
}

.vs-impact-grid span {
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.vs-impact-grid strong {
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 900;
}

.vs-impact-grid strong.sev { color: var(--red); }

.vs-disclaimer {
  margin-top: 12px;
  font-size: 8.5px;
  color: var(--muted);
  line-height: 1.5;
}

@media (max-width: 1100px) {
  .vstudio-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  .vs-stages { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); }
}

@media (max-width: 700px) {
  .vs-stage { display: none; }
  .vs-stages { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  .vs-map-wrap { height: 340px; }
}

/* --- Video Studio additions: region picker, science/error boxes --- */
#vsRegion,
.vs-config select {
  width: 100%;
  background: var(--surface-2, #131a24);
  color: var(--text, #dfe8f1);
  border: 1px solid var(--border, #26313d);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 8px 10px;
  font-size: 13px;
}
.vs-region-note {
  display: none;
  margin-top: 6px;
  font-size: 12px;
  line-height: 1.45;
  color: var(--muted, #9fb0c0);
}
.vs-region-note strong { color: var(--text, #dfe8f1); }
.vs-error-box {
  margin-top: 12px;
  padding: 12px 16px;
  border: 1px solid #7a2a24;
  border-left: 4px solid #e8382f;
  background: rgba(232, 56, 47, 0.08);
  color: #ffb4ad;
  border-radius: 10px;
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
}
```

### `vercel.json`  (15 lines)

```json
{
  "buildCommand": "npm run build",
  "outputDirectory": "dist",
  "framework": "vite",
  "rewrites": [
    {
      "source": "/api/(.*)",
      "destination": "https://aravindha-disaster-system.onrender.com/api/$1"
    },
    {
      "source": "/(.*)",
      "destination": "/index.html"
    }
  ]
}
```
